\doxysection{Eigen\+::Levenberg\+Marquardt\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} Functor\+Type, Scalar \texorpdfstring{$>$}{>} クラステンプレート}
\hypertarget{class_eigen_1_1_levenberg_marquardt}{}\label{class_eigen_1_1_levenberg_marquardt}\index{Eigen::LevenbergMarquardt$<$ FunctorType, Scalar $>$@{Eigen::LevenbergMarquardt$<$ FunctorType, Scalar $>$}}


Performs non linear optimization over a non-\/linear function, using a variant of the Levenberg Marquardt algorithm.  




{\ttfamily \#include $<$Levenberg\+Marquardt.\+h$>$}

\doxysubsection{詳解}
\subsubsection*{template$<$typename \mbox{\hyperlink{class_eigen_1_1_levenberg_marquardt_ae64d826e7c3560a2ad0b88316715e1df_ae64d826e7c3560a2ad0b88316715e1df}{Functor\+Type}}, typename \mbox{\hyperlink{class_eigen_1_1_levenberg_marquardt_a77419845705269de7f0e8aa37ceee6c7_a77419845705269de7f0e8aa37ceee6c7}{Scalar}} = double$>$\newline
class Eigen\+::\+Levenberg\+Marquardt$<$ Functor\+Type, Scalar $>$}
Performs non linear optimization over a non-\/linear function, using a variant of the Levenberg Marquardt algorithm. 

Check wikipedia for more information. \href{http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Levenberg\%E2\%80\%93Marquardt_algorithm}{\texttt{ http\+://en.\+wikipedia.\+org/wiki/\+Levenberg\%\+E2\%80\%93\+Marquardt\+\_\+algorithm}}

\begin{DoxyReturn}{戻り値}
{\ttfamily Success} if the minimization was successful, {\ttfamily Numerical\+Issue} if a numerical problem arises during the minimization process, for example during the QR factorization {\ttfamily No\+Convergence} if the minimization did not converge after the maximum number of function evaluation allowed {\ttfamily Invalid\+Input} if the input matrix is invalid 
\end{DoxyReturn}
